#### WR-V4-01 — WARNING: unknown-error classifier can throw before its withheld fallback

**File:** `/Users/roryquinlan/runtime/cowards-game/scripts/run-v1-38-lean-baseline.ts:898`; caller line943; relevant tests `/Users/roryquinlan/runtime/cowards-game/scripts/run-v1-38-lean-baseline.test.ts:74-77`.

**Issue:** `leanContinuationPreparationCodeV4(error: unknown)` performs `error instanceof TypeError` outside its try/catch. JavaScript's ordinary instanceof operation walks the left operand's prototype chain; a revoked Proxy raises TypeError, and a Proxy whose `getPrototypeOf` trap throws raises that exception. These unknown values therefore escape instead of returning the finite `DETAILS_WITHHELD` fallback. In preparation's catch at943, this can replace the original exception before the WeakMap entry is stored, losing the original stage/error identity and finite attribution. The existing own-message-getter test does not exercise this prototype operation. This is a bounded classifier/attribution robustness defect, not evidence of any observed preparation failure or private disclosure. The final formatter at903-905 only uses WeakMap.get and fixed UNKNOWN/DETAILS_WITHHELD fallback; the actual CLI at1087 still writes the unchanged withheld line. No raw/private CLI leak is established.

**Fix:** Move the instanceof check inside the existing try block, before own-data descriptor lookup, so both prototype inspection and message-descriptor failures return DETAILS_WITHHELD. Add direct inert classifier assertions for an explicitly revoked Proxy and a throwing-getPrototypeOf Proxy, plus fixed formatter fallback assertions; assert no thrown exception/raw content/getter invocation. Preserve the fixed stage/code enum, WeakMap attribution, exact V4-only CLI condition and unchanged V3 withheld output. Only driver/helper-test correction is necessary; no runtime/data/history scope expansion. This reviewer did not execute a reproducer or edit source; the static counterexamples follow the JavaScript instanceof/Proxy semantics.

### Narrative Findings (AI reviewer)

#### WR-OV5-01 — WARNING: alleged in-flight directory regression mutates before directory capture

**File:** `/Users/roryquinlan/runtime/cowards-game/scripts/lib/v1-38-lean-baseline-continuation.test.ts:329-332` (faulty directory branch at332).

**Issue:** The custom callback checks `f.guard.mock.calls.length === 0`, but supplying that callback replaces the default `f.guard`; it never invokes `f.guard`. Therefore the mutation runs at the first inventory guard, before any initial directory stat/member capture, and merely adds an already-invalid temp member. It does not replace a directory or exercise the newly added closing directory identity/member predicates. This test would still pass if the post-capture `V4_SPENT_CHANGED` directory checks were removed. The file-replacement and owner branches are separate and do not close this directory coverage gap. This is a test-reliability/false-boundary-coverage warning, not proof that production directory enforcement is broken.

**Fix:** Replace this branch with a connected post-capture regression. Wrap and still invoke `f.guard(additional)` on every callback; instrument real lstat/readdir phases for the exact spent V4 store/temp to prove initial identity and exact membership were captured before arming the mutation. Inject replacement or an added member only during the subsequent closing phase, assert the armed phase actually ran and require the specific closing refusal before inventory/debit use. Include a stable-synthetic-stat member-change case if asserting closing `readdir` independently, so an earlier timestamp failure cannot mask removal of that member check. Assert the original guard remains invoked throughout, and preserve production guard/descriptors/reservations. Use only the existing inert fixture/original helper test path; no history/capacity/provider/reader run is necessary. Re-review the actual corrected source and assertion delta after author closure.

### Narrative Findings (AI reviewer)

#### CR-V8-01 — BLOCKER: Parent reserve-only polling retains all36 cells after child progress

**File:** `/Users/roryquinlan/runtime/cowards-game/scripts/run-v1-38-lean-baseline.ts:925`, `:938`, `:973`, `:518`, `:1330`, `:1370`, `:1412`.

**Issue:** Each invocation-local reservation starts with `remainingSlots=36`; its only update is `setRemainingSlots` from the complete `sampleGuard`. The actual parent passes `c.guard` as `beforeRelease` and calls it every250ms while the child runs. For V8 this callback is now reserve-only, so it never updates the parent's count from the child's durable progress. Parent full stages run before release and after exit, not after each child Match; the child's separate controller/state cannot update this parent closure. Therefore the parent continues reserving `36*600000+1860000+600000` throughout execution and kills a still-progressing child as soon as actual time passes `A+4740000`, even if completed cells make the real remaining work fit. This is a source-level functional refusal, NOT a diagnosis of the original V7 throw or evidence that actual V8 capacity fits.

**Fix:** Refresh a bounded, authenticated, append-only owned-ledger progress frontier in the parent's reserve path, without full source/history recursion or trusting IPC hints as authority. Compute unfinished cells conservatively from durable charge/terminal state, INCLUDING any charged-but-not-terminal/in-flight cell; do not simply use `36-charges`, since the parent's `active` flag is not the child's. Preserve fresh wall/monotonic/time/capacity checks and anchor closure to actual terminal observation. Add a connected parent polling regression that advances past the original full36 latest-entry time after completed cells, still reserves a live charged cell, and rejects a truly insufficient remaining frontier. Keep all older route cadence unchanged.

#### CR-V8-02 — BLOCKER: Removed owned files escape per-path no-shrink checks when other files grow

**File:** `/Users/roryquinlan/runtime/cowards-game/scripts/lib/v1-38-lean-baseline-continuation.ts:554-576`; related subset-only boundary membership at `/Users/roryquinlan/runtime/cowards-game/scripts/run-v1-38-lean-baseline.ts:1271-1281`.

**Issue:** Owned measurement records previously observed paths in `previous`, but walks only the CURRENT directory members and never requires every previously observed member to remain present. The ENOENT check works only for paths actually visited. For example, after measuring `initial-analysis.json`, delete it and add/grow a larger allowed `current-analysis.json`. The removed file is never visited, the new path passes its allowlist, and net aggregate blocks can increase, defeating the accounting layer's aggregate monotonic check. Its already-spent blocks are silently omitted from current measured debit. Complete store membership likewise rejects extra names but does not reject a missing previously observed allowed artifact. A later reader may refuse missing evidence, but that does not restore no-refund capacity accounting before intervening charges.

**Fix:** After each owned traversal, require every previously observed path to have been seen again (and retain the existing per-path inode/mode/block no-shrink predicates). Keep initially absent prospective store/allocation paths legitimate until first observed; do not globally require every allowlisted future output. Permanently invalidate on disappearance, or retain an explicit non-refundable per-path debit with a separate integrity refusal. Add deletion-plus-compensating-growth regression and assert refusal before another reservation/charge; the current P test's name says shrink is covered, but its actual mutation only adds an unknown member.

### CR-01 — BLOCKER: full periodic guard still runs during the armed release wait

**File:** `scripts/run-v1-38-lean-baseline.ts:465` (related451,467,519,616,1519; child timer431).

**Issue:** The coordinator's250ms interval always calls `check()` with its default `fresh=true`, including phase `armed`. That invokes the real `checkWindow` callback, including `beforeRelease`/`c.guard`, reservation/descriptor/resource work and RSS observation. After `armRelease` is sent, a queued poll can start before the asynchronous acknowledgement is delivered; synchronous guard work can occupy the parent beyond the independently running child's30000ms release deadline. Thus healthy completed staging can still be followed by a false handshake timeout. The acknowledgement handler's `check(false)` does not prevent this competing interval path. This is a conditional source defect, NOT the proven original V8 cause. The connected fixture's synchronous acknowledgement cannot exercise this window.

**Minimal fix:** Preserve every complete fresh guard before arming. During the armed coordination phase, use only bounded connection/PID/exit/original-absolute-deadline checks (`check(false)` or equivalent), with no further synchronous full admission/reservation work before token delivery; retain existing post-release resource supervision and all complete stage checks. Do not extend, reset or remove the30000ms timer. Add a focused connected inert regression delaying the acknowledgement across a250ms poll: assert no full guard re-entry after arm and before token, while absolute expiry/disconnect still deny. ROOT owns repair and re-review; this review is not a source pass.

**Qualified coverage gap, not another demonstrated defect:** Executor reports23 new passing cases (A11+B9+C3),9.14s. The default-opening C fixture is explicitly `it.skip` after closed11.83/11.95s attempts exceeded its10s contract; reported real two-controller/one-importer fixed-anchor refusal is NOT passing valid-importer→claim→missing-result coverage.
